name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0

                                f"relative import for {name!r}")

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")

                level += 1

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)
E       ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'taskboard.inbox'

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0

            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':

name = 'taskboard.suggest', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:

                                f"relative import for {name!r}")

                if character != '.':

                level += 1

name = 'taskboard.suggest', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)
E       ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'taskboard.suggest'

        first); `Beta` before `beta` (equal fold, then the stored name; NC position first puts

name = 'taskboard.suggest', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1

    async def test_TC_4411_capture_is_refused_while_a_modal_is_open(home):
        """TC-4411 (LLR-4401.11): `check_action("capture", ())` False with a ModalScreen (the task

name = 'taskboard.suggest', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)
E       ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'taskboard.inbox'

    def test_TC_4509_another_board_is_written_fresh_under_its_lock(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
        """TC-4509 (LLR-4404.9, layer 0): at the save the board's lock file exists; `save_atomic` is
        called once and `Board.save` never on that board; a task another writer appended to the file
        before the dump is still there after it (3/3; `move_project`'s pattern, R1-3)."""
        p, ids = s_dump(tmp_path, monkeypatch)

                              "phase": "Todo"})
        p["C"].write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        lock = p["C"].resolve().with_name("board.json.lock")
        atomic, plain = [], []
        real_atomic, real_save = Board.save_atomic, Board.save
    
        def spy_atomic(self):
            atomic.append((Path(self.path).resolve(), lock.exists()))
            return real_atomic(self)
    
        def spy_save(self):
            plain.append(Path(self.path).resolve())

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)
E       ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'taskboard.inbox'

    def test_TC_4523b_a_new_project_dump_is_one_save_on_either_path(tmp_path, monkeypatch):

        another board → `save_atomic` once; on both the task's project_id is the new project's."""
        p, ids = s_dump(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
        calls = {"save": 0, "atomic": 0, "add_project": 0}
        real_save, real_atomic, real_add = Board.save, Board.save_atomic, Board.add_project
    
        def count(name, real):
            def f(self, *a):
                calls[name] += 1
                return real(self, *a)
            return f
        monkeypatch.setattr(Board, "save", count("save", real_save))
        monkeypatch.setattr(Board, "save_atomic", count("atomic", real_atomic))
        monkeypatch.setattr(Board, "add_project", count("add_project", real_add))

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)
E       ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'taskboard.inbox'

name = 'taskboard.inbox', package = None

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
>       return _bootstrap._gcd_import(name[level:], package, level)

    def import_module(name, package=None):
        """Import a module.
    
        The 'package' argument is required when performing a relative import. It
        specifies the package to use as the anchor point from which to resolve the
        relative import to an absolute import.
    
        """
        level = 0
        if name.startswith('.'):
            if not package:
                raise TypeError("the 'package' argument is required to perform a "
                                f"relative import for {name!r}")
            for character in name:
                if character != '.':
                    break
                level += 1
